# Known rows that byte-match while naming the wrong function.
# Read by tools/identity_guard.py (commit hook) -- see that file for the rules.
#
# These numbers only go DOWN. Raising one to make a commit pass converts a found
# defect into a permanent one, exactly as raising ORPHAN_BASELINE would.
#
# multi_name.family     two accessors one family member apart sharing a body:
#                       what a shared layout error looks like. Undecided, not
#                       proven -- see docs/lessons.md on FAMILY flags.
# multi_name.different  bodies that cannot share an address at all.
# size_outlier.indicted a forwarder-shaped body whose callers place it elsewhere.
# ctor_vtable.contradicted a constructor row whose body installs a vtable that
#                       names a different class. multi_name clears these as a
#                       structural fold and is right to: build.py masks the
#                       vftable operand, so they really are one shape. 39 of the
#                       47 share an address with another constructor name; the
#                       group of 12 at 0x0024FC20 is gone -- those names are not
#                       in BFME's module-name string pool at all, so the rows
#                       were deleted (targets/game/reverse/deleted_rows.csv) rather than fixed.
# one_identity.surplus  non-placeholder names beyond the first at one address.
#                       Retail was linked without identical-COMDAT folding
#                       (evidence: python3 tools/one_identity.py), so each is an
#                       over-claim; --list prints the addresses. Names, not
#                       addresses, so a third name on a two-name body counts.
# ctor_vtable.unreadable a coverage floor like null_reloc.max_unreadable, not a
#                       defect count. Zero, and it stays zero.
#
# null_reloc.* is read by the FULL GATE, not the hook: that sweep takes ~70s.
# max_unreadable is a coverage floor, not a defect count. A row the sweep cannot
# read is one it reports clean without looking, so the scan is only meaningful
# while nearly every row is readable: a fully built tree leaves 394 unreadable,
# a tree whose objects were never built leaves 118,251 -- and prints the same 67
# findings either way. Raising this limit to get green disables the check.
multi_name.family = 0
multi_name.different = 0
size_outlier.indicted = 1
ctor_vtable.contradicted = 20
ctor_vtable.unreadable = 0
one_identity.surplus = 2226
# Retail registration proves DynamicPortalBehaviour owns ctor 001F8B80 and factory 001156B0; four false aliases retired.
# fcf825b4f7 retired the false FXNugget zero-store claim; the body still has aliases.
# 4c8346b3c9 retired the GDIFileStream ctor over-claim at 0x00087A50 (a zero-store body).
null_reloc.rows = 50
null_reloc.bodies = 18
null_reloc.max_unreadable = 1000
